HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000051/B001","root_000051/B003","root_000051/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_082a532681a67eb1900f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:12:2\",\"root_000051\",[\"B001\",\"B003\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Buyruk kökünün iş, yetki ve danışma çağrışımları, ayeti özel bir öğüdün ötesinde hangi toplumsal karar düzeninin geçerli olduğu sorusuna açar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001677/B001","root_001677/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1412ade897a3ef7ef904","text":"96:13’s turning away sharpened the guarded-gait image `و ق ي:B003`: taqwa is careful continuance where the alternative leaves the course. The forelock threat and council call in 96:15–17 then made `و ق ي:B001` a social barrier against capture, and 96:17 made the authority branch `ء م ر:B003` newly relevant by displaying the rival’s own mobilized command structure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000051/B001","root_000051/B002","root_001486/B001","root_001677/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_534e8478a6b848edb1f1","text":"1. Command is reclaimed from the transgressor: the servant may be one who commands protective God-consciousness. Evidence: `ء م ر B002` command/directive, `و ق ي B002` self-protective caution/taqwa, `و ق ي B001` protective barrier. Structural cue: this ayah answers the illegitimate `ينهى` with a legitimate `أمر`. Mechanism: true command does not block worship; it places the self behind a protective moral barrier. Abductive move: the packet supplies command and protection; I infer that taqwa is the content that distinguishes rightful command from coercive prohibition. Reading change: the ayah reads as a counter-authority scene: perhaps the forbidden servant is actually directing protection. 2. `أمر` also activates affair, consultation, and authority. Evidence: `ء م ر B001` matter/affair, `ء م ر B003` authority/command-holder, `ء م ر B007` consultation/deliberation; later `ن د و B001` council gathering. Structural cue: the human opponent later calls his council in 96:17. Mechanism: the passage contrasts a command toward protection with a council-backed command toward obstruction. Abductive move: the packet gives `أمر` and later `ناديه`; I infer a social governance mechanism. Reading change: the ayah is not private advice only; it points to which social command-system should govern the affair. 3. Taqwa activates bodily guarded movement. Evidence: `و ق ي B003` guarded gait from hoof pain and `ق ر ب B007/B008` careful approach/water-seeking. Structural cue: the final ayah commands approach, but only after “do not obey him.” Mechanism: protective caution is not paralysis; it regulates how one moves near. Abductive move: the packet gives guard/protection and later approach; I infer that taqwa is disciplined movement toward God while avoiding harmful obedience. Reading change: `بالتقوى` becomes active protective orientation, not abstract piety.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_67979429821867acceec","text":"96:19 made this command concrete: taqwa becomes “do not obey him; prostrate; draw near.” The protective barrier is enacted as refusal of a rival command.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000051/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6ed8304d484f2bc28906","text":"1. **A directive that constructs protection.** `ء م ر:B002` supplies command or directive, and `و ق ي:B002` supplies placing the self in protective moral caution. The directive stands opposite the restrainer’s `ينهى` and is followed by denial, withdrawal, and threatened seizure (96:13–16). The fixed roots assign two roles: `أمر` transmits an obligation, while `التقوى` is the protective state that obligation seeks to produce. The packet supplies the contrast; I infer that compliance builds a guard against the ensuing harmful course. The ayah reads as active protection-making rather than a vague commendation of piety.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001677/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_81980bda456382db9b86","text":"3. **Taqwa as a barrier against coercive harm.** `و ق ي:B001` supplies a guard or barrier placed between something and injury. Forelock seizure, the lying/wrongdoing characterization, and the council call all lie ahead inside the window (96:15–17). The barrier branch’s role is to make `التقوى` materially defensive: it keeps the commanded person from being absorbed into the denier’s chain of obedience. The packet does not specify the barrier’s mechanism; I infer that guarded conduct blocks moral capture. The noun reads as preserved agency under pressure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000051/B002","root_000051/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c1401c7beb1418c094bd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:12:2\",\"root_000051\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Doğru buyruğun koruyucu otoritesi, namazı engelleyen kişinin rakip buyurma düzeninin hangi yetkiye dayanabileceğini sorgulatır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000051/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c3517b2e03ca32ee1a48","text":"2. **Legitimate command confronting rival command.** `ء م ر:B003` supplies authority and the command-holder. One figure forbids prayer (96:9–10); the alternative subject commands protective caution; the antagonist later calls his council (96:17). The authority branch turns `أمر` into a jurisdictional question: which directive deserves social force? I infer from the opposed commands that guidance authorizes the protective directive while self-sufficient transgression produces the rival one. The line therefore contests governance, not only private moral advice.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001677/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e143f556f9e4df52d5d2","text":"4. **Careful movement along guidance.** `و ق ي:B003` supplies a guarded gait that protects a painful hoof from rough ground. The preceding ayah places the subject `على الهدى`, whose `ه د ي:B002` is a directed course, while 96:13 supplies turning away. The gait branch gives taqwa a locomotor role: proceed attentively so that the path does not injure or divert the walker. I infer the path-and-gait analogy from adjacency. The ayah can thus describe disciplined movement, not static fear.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001677/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e97b2014479d8d879091","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:12:3\",\"root_001677\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sakınma, doğru yol üzerinde incinmeden ve sapmadan ilerlemek için adımlarını dikkatle atan kişinin korunaklı yürüyüşü gibi etkin bir yöneliştir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000051/B002","root_001677/B001","root_001677/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f5ba5287779374e3d0ea","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:12:2\",\"root_000051\",[\"B002\"]],[\"96:12:3\",\"root_001677\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sakınmayı buyurmak, soyut bir öğüt vermek değil, insanı zararlı itaate ve ahlaki yakalanmaya karşı koruyan bir engel kurmaktır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
